 547: const confirmHandler: CustomerHttpHandler = async (req, _ctx, customer) => {
 548:   const id = (req as unknown as { params: { id: string } }).params.id;
 549:   const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
 550:   const parsed = ConfirmBookingRequestSchema.safeParse(body);
 551:   if (!parsed.success) return { status: 422, jsonBody: { code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR', issues: parsed.error.issues } };
 552: 
 553:   const booking = await bookingRepo.getById(id);
 554:   if (!booking) return { status: 404, jsonBody: { code: 'BOOKING_NOT_FOUND' } };
 555:   if (booking.customerId !== customer.customerId) return { status: 403, jsonBody: { code: 'FORBIDDEN' } };
 556: 
 557:   if (!verifyPaymentSignature({
 558:     razorpayOrderId: parsed.data.razorpayOrderId,
 559:     razorpayPaymentId: parsed.data.razorpayPaymentId,
 560:     razorpaySignature: parsed.data.razorpaySignature,
 561:   })) return { status: 400, jsonBody: { code: 'SIGNATURE_INVALID' } };
 562: 
 563:   const confirmed = await bookingRepo.confirmPayment(id, parsed.data.razorpayPaymentId, parsed.data.razorpaySignature);
 564:   if (!confirmed) return { status: 409, jsonBody: { code: 'BOOKING_ALREADY_PROCESSED' } };
 565: 
 566:   // Only audit when this call actually performed the transition. If status is PAID the webhook
 567:   // already processed the booking â€” this is an idempotent confirm, not a new event.
 568:   if (confirmed.status === 'SEARCHING') {
 569:     const _ts = new Date().toISOString();
 570:     void appendAuditEntry({ id: randomUUID(), adminId: 'system', role: 'system', action: 'CUSTOMER_CONFIRMED_PAYMENT', resourceType: 'booking', resourceId: confirmed.id, payload: { bookingId: confirmed.id, paymentId: parsed.data.razorpayPaymentId }, timestamp: _ts, partitionKey: _ts.slice(0, 7) }).catch(Sentry.captureException);
 571:   }
 572: 
 573:   return { status: 200, jsonBody: { bookingId: confirmed.id, status: confirmed.status } };
 574: };
 575: 
 576: export const createBookingHandler: HttpHandler = requireCustomer(createHandler);
 577: export const confirmBookingHandler: HttpHandler = requireCustomer(confirmHandler);
 578: 
 579: const getBookingInner: CustomerHttpHandler = async (req, _ctx, customer) => {
 580:   const id = (req as unknown as { params: { id: string } }).params.id;
 581:   const booking = await bookingRepo.getById(id);
 582:   if (!booking) return { status: 404, jsonBody: { code: 'BOOKING_NOT_FOUND' } };
 583:   if (booking.customerId !== customer.customerId) return { status: 403, jsonBody: { code: 'FORBIDDEN' } };

 590:       approvedAddOns: booking.approvedAddOns ?? [],

 593: };
 594: export const getBookingHandler: HttpHandler = requireCustomer(getBookingInner);

  27: export const bookingRepo = {
  28:   async createPending(
  29:     req: CreateBookingRequest,

  35:     creditOptions?: BookingCreateCreditOptions,
  36:   ): Promise<BookingDoc> {
  37:     const paymentMethod = req.paymentMethod ?? 'RAZORPAY';

  40:       addressText: normalizeAddressText(req.addressText),
  41:       ...(metadata.customerName ? { customerName: metadata.customerName } : {}),
  42:       ...(metadata.customerPhone ? { customerPhone: metadata.customerPhone } : {}),
  43:       ...(metadata.customerEmail ? { customerEmail: metadata.customerEmail } : {}),
  44:       ...(metadata.serviceName ? { serviceName: metadata.serviceName } : {}),
  45:       status: 'PENDING_PAYMENT', paymentOrderId,

  55:           }
  56:         : {}),
  57:     };
  58:     const { resource } = await getBookingsContainer().items.create<BookingDoc>(doc);

 107:     if (!existing || (existing.status !== 'SEARCHING' && existing.status !== 'PENDING_PAYMENT')) return null;

 133:   },
 134: 
 135:   async getBookingsAwaitingDispatch(limit = 100): Promise<BookingDoc[]> {
 136:     const { resources } = await getBookingsContainer().items.query<BookingDoc>({
 137:       query: `SELECT * FROM c
 138:               WHERE c.status IN ('PAID', 'UNFULFILLED')
 139:                 AND (NOT IS_DEFINED(c.technicianId) OR IS_NULL(c.technicianId))`,
 140:       parameters: [],
 141:     }).fetchAll();
 142:     return resources.slice(0, limit);
 143:   },
 144: 
 145:   async getAssignedBookingsBefore(slotDateCutoff: string): Promise<BookingDoc[]> {
 146:     const { resources } = await getBookingsContainer()
 147:       .items.query<BookingDoc>({
 148:         query: "SELECT * FROM c WHERE (c.status IN ('ASSIGNED', 'NO_SHOW_REDISPATCH') OR (c.status = 'SEARCHING' AND IS_DEFINED(c.noShowTechnicianId))) AND c.slotDate <= @slotDate",
 149:         parameters: [{ name: '@slotDate', value: slotDateCutoff }],
 150:       })
 151:       .fetchAll();
 152:     return resources;
 153:   },
 154: 
 155:   async getByTechnicianId(technicianId: string): Promise<BookingDoc[]> {
 156:     const { resources } = await getBookingsContainer()
 157:       .items.query<BookingDoc>({
 158:         query: `SELECT * FROM c
 159:                 WHERE c.technicianId = @technicianId
 160:                   AND c.status IN (
 161:                     'ASSIGNED', 'EN_ROUTE', 'REACHED', 'IN_PROGRESS',
 162:                     'AWAITING_PRICE_APPROVAL', 'COMPLETED', 'PAID', 'CLOSED'
 163:                   )`,
 164:         parameters: [{ name: '@technicianId', value: technicianId }],
 165:       })
 166:       .fetchAll();
 167:     // Sort in memory â€” ORDER BY on a cross-partition (non-PK) query requires
 168:     // a composite index that isn't provisioned on the bookings container.
 169:     // Sort in-memory after the composite index is provisioned (see
 170:     // scripts/provision-cosmos-indexes.ts). The index covers [/technicianId,
 171:     // /slotDate, /slotWindow] so ORDER BY in the query is also valid, but
 172:     // in-memory sort keeps this function safe even before the first index rebuild.
 173:     return resources.sort(
 174:       (a, b) =>
 175:         a.slotDate.localeCompare(b.slotDate) || a.slotWindow.localeCompare(b.slotWindow),
 176:     );
 177:   },
 178: 
 179:   async getByCustomerId(customerId: string): Promise<BookingDoc[]> {
 180:     const { resources } = await getBookingsContainer()
 181:       .items.query<BookingDoc>({
 182:         query: `SELECT * FROM c
 183:                 WHERE c.customerId = @customerId`,
 184:         parameters: [{ name: '@customerId', value: customerId }],
 185:       })
 186:       .fetchAll();
 187:     return resources.sort((a, b) => {
 188:       const slotCompare = b.slotDate.localeCompare(a.slotDate) || b.slotWindow.localeCompare(a.slotWindow);
 189:       return slotCompare || b.createdAt.localeCompare(a.createdAt);
 190:     });
 191:   },
 192: 
 193:   async requestAddOn(id: string, addOn: PendingAddOn): Promise<BookingDoc | null> {
 194:     const existing = await this.getById(id);
 195:     if (!existing || existing.status !== 'IN_PROGRESS') return null;
 196:     const updated: BookingDoc = {
 197:       ...existing,
 198:       status: 'AWAITING_PRICE_APPROVAL',
 199:       pendingAddOns: [...(existing.pendingAddOns ?? []), addOn],
 200:       // Stable anchor for the ADDON_APPROVAL_REQUESTED pending-action expiry.
 201:       // Written atomically with the status transition so the change-feed projector
 202:       // can derive 24h from the actual request time (not the booking createdAt).
 203:       pendingAddOnsUpdatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 204:     };
 205:     const { resource } = await getBookingsContainer().item(id, id).replace<BookingDoc>(updated);
 206:     return resource!;
 207:   },
 208: 
 209:   async applyAddOnDecisions(id: string, customerId: string, decisions: AddOnDecision[]): Promise<BookingDoc | null> {
 210:     const existing = await this.getById(id);
 211:     if (!existing || existing.customerId !== customerId) return null;
 212:     if (existing.status !== 'AWAITING_PRICE_APPROVAL') return null;
 213:     const pending = existing.pendingAddOns ?? [];
 214:     const approved = pending.filter(a => decisions.find(d => d.name === a.name && d.approved));
 215:     const updated: BookingDoc = {
 216:       ...existing,
 217:       status: 'IN_PROGRESS',
 218:       pendingAddOns: [],
 219:       approvedAddOns: [...(existing.approvedAddOns ?? []), ...approved],
 220:       finalAmount: (existing.finalAmount ?? existing.amount) + approved.reduce((s, a) => s + a.price, 0),
 221:     };
 222:     const { resource } = await getBookingsContainer().item(id, id).replace<BookingDoc>(updated);
 223:     return resource!;
 224:   },
 225: 
 226:   async addPhoto(
 227:     bookingId: string,
 228:     stage: string,
 229:     photoUrl: string,
 230:   ): Promise<BookingDoc | null> {
 231:     const { resource: existing, etag } = await getBookingsContainer()
 232:       .item(bookingId, bookingId)
 233:       .read<BookingDoc>();
 234:     if (!existing) return null;
 235:     const stagePhotos = existing.photos?.[stage] ?? [];
 236:     const updated: BookingDoc = {
 237:       ...existing,
 238:       photos: { ...existing.photos, [stage]: [...stagePhotos, photoUrl] },
 239:     };
 240:     // Use ETag optimistic concurrency so concurrent uploads for the same
 241:     // booking/stage don't silently drop each other's photo URL.
 242:     const { resource } = await getBookingsContainer()
 243:       .item(bookingId, bookingId)
 244:       .replace<BookingDoc>(updated, { accessCondition: { type: 'IfMatch', condition: etag ?? '' } });
 245:     return resource ?? null;
 246:   },
 247: 
 248:   async markSosActivated(id: string): Promise<BookingDoc | null> {
 249:     const { resource: existing, etag } = await getBookingsContainer().item(id, id).read<BookingDoc>();
 250:     if (!existing) return null;
 251:     if (existing.sosActivatedAt) return existing; // already activated â€” concurrent request lost the race
 252:     const updated: BookingDoc = { ...existing, sosActivatedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
 253:     try {
 254:       const { resource } = await getBookingsContainer()
 255:         .item(id, id)
 256:         .replace<BookingDoc>(updated, { accessCondition: { type: 'IfMatch', condition: etag ?? '' } });
 257:       return resource ?? null;
 258:     } catch (e: unknown) {
 259:       if (typeof e === 'object' && e !== null && 'code' in e && (e as { code: number }).code === 412) {
 260:         return null; // lost ETag race â€” caller handles as already-activated
 261:       }
 262:       throw e;
 263:     }
 264:   },
 265: 
 266:   // E16-S02: Returns slotWindow strings for all active (non-cancelled/unfulfilled) bookings
 267:   // for a given service on a given date. Used by the availability handler to mark slots
 268:   // as hard-booked. Cross-partition scan â€” acceptable at pilot scale (â‰¤5,000 bookings/mo).
 269:   async getBookedWindowsByServiceDate(serviceId: string, date: string): Promise<string[]> {
 270:     const { resources } = await getBookingsContainer()
 271:       .items.query<{ slotWindow: string }>({
 272:         query: `SELECT c.slotWindow FROM c
 273:                 WHERE c.serviceId = @serviceId
 274:                   AND c.slotDate = @date
 275:                   AND c.status NOT IN ('CUSTOMER_CANCELLED', 'UNFULFILLED')`,
 276:         parameters: [
 277:           { name: '@serviceId', value: serviceId },
 278:           { name: '@date', value: date },
 279:         ],
 280:       })
 281:       .fetchAll();
 282:     return resources.map((r) => r.slotWindow);
 283:   },
 284: };

+  // E16-S02: Returns slotWindow strings for all active (non-cancelled/unfulfilled) bookings
+  // for a given service on a given date. Used by the availability handler to mark slots
+  // as hard-booked. Cross-partition scan â€” acceptable at pilot scale (â‰¤5,000 bookings/mo).
+  async getBookedWindowsByServiceDate(serviceId: string, date: string): Promise<string[]> {
+    const { resources } = await getBookingsContainer()
+      .items.query<{ slotWindow: string }>({
+        query: `SELECT c.slotWindow FROM c
+                WHERE c.serviceId = @serviceId
+                  AND c.slotDate = @date
+                  AND c.status NOT IN ('CUSTOMER_CANCELLED', 'UNFULFILLED')`,
+        parameters: [
+          { name: '@serviceId', value: serviceId },
+          { name: '@date', value: date },
+        ],
+      })
+      .fetchAll();
+    return resources.map((r) => r.slotWindow);
+  },
 };

+/**
+ * E16-S02: Slot holds for conflict locking.
+ * Partitioned by /servicePartitionKey ("<serviceId>|<date>"). Default TTL = 30 s.
+ * commitHold sets ttl=-1 on committed docs to make them permanent.
+ */
+export function getSlotHoldsContainer(): Container {
+  return getCosmosClient().database(DB_NAME).container('slot_holds');
+}

diff --git a/api/src/cosmos/slot-holds-repository.ts b/api/src/cosmos/slot-holds-repository.ts

+++ b/api/src/cosmos/slot-holds-repository.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,81 @@
+import { getSlotHoldsContainer } from './client.js';
+import type { SlotHoldDoc } from '../schemas/slot-hold.js';
+
+export class SlotHoldsRepository {
+  private get container() { return getSlotHoldsContainer(); }
+
+  /**
+   * Attempts to create a soft hold for the given slot.
+   * Returns the hold doc on success, or 'CONFLICT' if the slot is already held
+   * (Cosmos 409) or there is an optimistic-concurrency conflict (Cosmos 412 â€”
+   * same fallback pattern as ADR-0017 Â§5 wallet etag).
+   */
+  async createHold(
+    serviceId: string,
+    date: string,
+    window: string,
+    customerId: string,
+  ): Promise<SlotHoldDoc | 'CONFLICT'> {
+    const id = `${serviceId}|${date}|${window}`;
+    const servicePartitionKey = `${serviceId}|${date}`;
+    // Note: ttl is intentionally omitted â€” the container default (30 s) applies automatically.
+    // exactOptionalPropertyTypes=true means we can't pass ttl?: number | undefined to create<T>.
+    const doc = {
+      id,
+      servicePartitionKey,
+      serviceId,
+      date,
+      window,
+      customerId,
+      heldAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+    } satisfies Omit<SlotHoldDoc, 'bookingId' | 'ttl'>;
+
+    try {
+      const { resource } = await this.container.items.create(doc);
+      return resource as SlotHoldDoc;
+    } catch (err: unknown) {
+      const code = (err as { statusCode?: number }).statusCode;
+      if (code === 409 || code === 412) return 'CONFLICT';
+      throw err;
+    }
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Converts a soft hold to a permanent booking-owned slot record.

   7:   if (!_rzp) {
   8:     const keyId = process.env.RAZORPAY_KEY_ID;
   9:     const keySecret = process.env.RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET;
  10:     if (!keyId || !keySecret) throw new Error('Missing RAZORPAY_KEY_ID or RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET');
  11:     _rzp = new Razorpay({ key_id: keyId, key_secret: keySecret });
  12:   }
  13:   return _rzp;
  14: }
  15: 
  16: export async function createRazorpayOrder(opts: {
  17:   amount: number;
  18:   currency: string;
  19:   receipt: string;
  20:   notes?: Record<string, string>;
  21: }) {
  22:   const order = await getRazorpay().orders.create(opts);
  23:   return { id: order.id, amount: order.amount, currency: order.currency };
  24: }
  25: 
  26: export function verifyPaymentSignature(opts: {
  27:   razorpayOrderId: string;
  28:   razorpayPaymentId: string;
  29:   razorpaySignature: string;
  30: }): boolean {
  31:   const secret = process.env.RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET;
  32:   if (!secret) throw new Error('Missing RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET');
  33:   return equalsHexHmac(
  34:     secret,
  35:     `${opts.razorpayOrderId}|${opts.razorpayPaymentId}`,
  36:     opts.razorpaySignature,
  37:   );
  38: }
  39: 
  40: export async function createTransfer(opts: {
  41:   accountId: string;
  42:   amount: number;
  43:   currency?: string;
  44:   notes?: Record<string, string>;
  45:   idempotencyKey?: string;
  46: }): Promise<{ transferId: string }> {
  47:   const result = await (getRazorpay().transfers.create as (
  48:     params: unknown,
  49:     callOpts?: unknown,
  50:   ) => Promise<{ id: string }>)(
  51:     {
  52:       account: opts.accountId,
  53:       amount: opts.amount,
  54:       currency: opts.currency ?? 'INR',
  55:       on_hold: 0,
  56:       ...(opts.notes && { notes: opts.notes }),
  57:     },
  58:     opts.idempotencyKey
  59:       ? { headers: { 'X-Razorpay-Idempotency-Key': opts.idempotencyKey } }
  60:       : undefined,
  61:   );
  62:   return { transferId: result.id };
  63: }

2026-05-18T06:15:35.466166Z ERROR codex_core::session: failed to record rollout items: thread 019e39af-a1bf-7d52-bae6-0027d76ea8af not found
codex
The slot-locking lifecycle can permanently block unpaid Razorpay reservations, and booking creation does not enforce the same date bounds as the new availability endpoint. The API contract and date validation also need follow-up fixes for the new route.

Full review comments:

- [P1] Keep Razorpay holds temporary until payment succeeds — C:/Alok/Business Projects/Urbanclap-dup/api/src/functions/bookings.ts:512-513
  In the Razorpay path this runs immediately after `createPending`, while the booking is still `PENDING_PAYMENT`. If the customer closes or fails the payment flow, there is no transition/delete for that pending booking, yet `commitHold` writes `ttl=-1` and the new booked-window check also treats `PENDING_PAYMENT` as occupied, so the slot stays unavailable and future creates return 409 indefinitely. Keep the hold expiring until payment is captured/confirmed, or add cleanup that releases unpaid reservations.

- [P2] Enforce the availability date range on booking creation — C:/Alok/Business Projects/Urbanclap-dup/api/src/functions/bookings.ts:155-158
  When `slotDate` is any past date other than today's IST date, or a future date outside the 7-day availability window, this code still calls `generateSlots` and accepts any matching window before creating a hold. Since the new availability endpoint rejects those dates, callers can bypass it by posting directly to `/v1/bookings`; apply the same today-to-today+7 IST range validation here before slot generation.

- [P2] Register the availability route in OpenAPI — C:/Alok/Business Projects/Urbanclap-dup/api/src/functions/services-availability.ts:78-81
  The function route is registered, but the regenerated `api/openapi.json` and generated TypeScript schema contain no `/v1/services/{id}/availability` operation. Any client generated from this repo's contract cannot discover or type the new endpoint; register it in the OpenAPI registry using `AvailabilityResponseSchema` and regenerate.

- [P3] Reject normalized invalid calendar dates — C:/Alok/Business Projects/Urbanclap-dup/api/src/functions/services-availability.ts:14-17
  Dates such as `2026-02-30` pass this refinement because JavaScript normalizes them to March 2 instead of producing `NaN`, so near a short-month boundary the endpoint can return slots for a non-existent date that is lexically within the allowed range. Validate the parsed year/month/day against the original string or use a stricter date parser.
